# TEST-MATRIX — executed coverage ledger

**Branch:** tester-final. **Baseline:** 4d4ea0964dfc6671cd76bc24d1e2bf96c401f821. Local isolated adapter suites and read-only staging smoke checks have executed; individual rows retain their evidence/status labels. STEP 0 build/lint/type-check results are recorded separately in BEFORE.md.

## Scope and decision gates

User authorized execution for isolated localhost setup and QA_TEST_ accounts. No production testing, schema/data modifications, deletion, push or deployment occurred. Required decisions are in DECISIONS.md; local isolation and real-provider blockers are in LOCAL-TEST-PLAN.md.

## Inventory summary

- 54 JSX route declarations -> 53 normalized route/guard templates, including wildcard containers. These are not 53 independently rendered pages.
- 902 UI source templates across 76 files: buttons/icons/links/forms/inputs/select options/dialogs/tabs/drags/images/video/QR/shared components. Every row below has an ID, source and expected source contract.
- 112 feature/nonfunctional case families, plus SQL object/policy IDs and four bucket IDs.
- Backend inventory: 10 HTTP handlers/actions, 33 public tables, 49 function names, 18 historical trigger names (one later dropped), 1 view, 4 buckets, 25 timestamped migrations. Policy list is historical; only an isolated catalog replay can establish the effective policies.
- 1320 indexed base rows in TEST-MATRIX.csv. Semantic/admin/integration annexes add detailed assertions; counts are not unique executed tests and must not be summed as coverage.

## Execution dimensions and status rules

1. Every applicable UI case expands to **1440/768/375 × EN/LTR and AR/RTL**. Exercise each visitor/member/admin identity separately; positive legacy roles require DEC-001. Also include member A/B, admin A/B, unconfirmed, pending/suspended, expired, missing profile and forged role requests.
2. Runtime case IDs use BASE-ID__role__locale__width__variant. Repeated rows/options/categories/question types/permissions/action dropdowns must each get a named variant from QA_TEST_ fixtures; source templates alone do not prove rendered coverage. Shared controls are exercised in every owning route/context.
3. **PASS** requires trigger + observed UI and relevant request/status/payload + actual local DB/file/redirect/email result. Annotate SQL-only, handler-adapter, browser-adapter and real-provider results separately. Adapter pass is never a real Google/Auth/Storage/realtime pass.
4. Record FAIL with sanitized screenshot/trace/network and before/after rows, bug ID and expected/actual. Blocked and Needs decision are separate from FAIL. Not applicable needs source/role evidence and explicit explanation. No silent skips.
5. Each error/empty/loading state and every form boundary (required/invalid/type/limit/limit+1/Unicode/HTML/script/URL) applies to each corresponding UI-ID. Every write also gets double-click/concurrent/lost-response/retry/denied-identity cases.
6. Every API action gets method/body/auth/role/IDOR/oversize/rate/concurrency/timeout/provider-failure cases. Every table gets SQL grants+RLS own/foreign CRUD, FK/check/unique/trigger/cascade/rollback tests. Historical dropped objects must be verified absent and safely unreachable.
7. No deletion test, including delete buttons/cleanup, runs until exact disposable local fixtures and recovery/deletion approval satisfy the user's deletion rule. Do not accidentally delete pre-existing real data or unused source files.
8. Each confirmed bug gets one fix(BUG-NNN) commit after targeted+related checks and build/lint/type-check. Any check failure stops the fix loop. Full matrix rerun precedes final approval.
9. Record all evidence under final/evidence using redacted metadata only. Never store access/refresh tokens, full OAuth/session URLs, API keys, private emails or secret file content in committed logs.
10. Last delivery gates: requested BEFORE/AFTER metrics, BUGS.md/bugs.csv, cleanup approvals, npm-ci/fresh-clone check and docs/license decisions. **Final report approval is required before any merge/push/deploy.**

## Feature, boundary and non-functional checklist

| ID | Page / role | Element / endpoint | Expected result | Status | Source |

# Annex: Admin semantic detail

# Admin UI source inventory — STEP 1 (not executed)

Scope: `src/pages/AdminDashboard.tsx`, every `src/pages/admin/**` page, every `src/components/admin/**` component, and their shared image/identity controls. This is a source inspection only: no application, browser, API, database, or integration tests have been executed for this inventory. Expected outcomes below describe implemented intent, not verified behavior. Every row remains **Pending approval / Not run**. References are repository-relative `file:line` locations.

## Role model and route boundaries

- **ADM-GUARD-01** `/admin/*`: `src/App.tsx:101` ManagerRoute requires a signed-in, confirmed, active profile and `isManager`; otherwise login/dashboard redirects or status feedback. Proposed: visitor, active member, inactive admin, unconfirmed admin, expired session, profile-load failure, direct deep link and refresh.
- **ADM-GUARD-02** nested routes and super-admin bypass: `src/pages/AdminDashboard.tsx:26` RoleGuard; all 14 route destinations are listed below. Proposed: direct URL/API authorization, hidden navigation, history-back after logout, refresh/session expiry while a modal is open.
- **ADM-GUARD-03** frontend has exactly **two roles**: `super_admin` and `member` (`src/types/index.ts:9`, `src/lib/roleDefinitions.ts:40`). All `isAdmin`, `isEditor`, `isHR`, `isEventManager`, `isContentManager`, `isFinanceManager`, `isOrganizer`, `isManager` alias `isSuperAdmin` (`src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx:487`). UI role normalization maps legacy DB admin into super_admin (`src/pages/admin/UserManagement.tsx:32`). Proposed: verify normalization and no false escalation from user metadata.
- **ADM-GUARD-04** database constraint is `profiles.role IN ('user','admin')` (`supabase/migrations/20261010000004_phase2_schema_hardening.sql:52`); user management converts member→user, super_admin→admin (`src/pages/admin/UserManagement.tsx:36`). Server admin endpoints require verified email, active profile, admin/super_admin role (`api/_lib/authorize.ts:4`, `api/_lib/authorize.ts:30`). Proposed: RPC/RLS restrictions and identity-vs-payload trust checks in isolated database.
- **ADM-DECISION-01 — Needs decision:** requested distinct admin/hr/event_manager/content_manager/editor test accounts cannot be assigned distinct existing privileges: those roles do not exist in the current type, role picker, or DB role constraint. Test supported member/super_admin and deny malformed unsupported role payloads; do not implement or silently infer new privileges. Ask whether the two-role design is the accepted product model or a future six-role implementation is required.
- **ADM-DECISION-02 — Needs decision:** role descriptions/permission cards are display metadata, not independently editable ACLs (`src/lib/roleDefinitions.ts:12`, `src/components/admin/RoleEditorModal.tsx:205`). Member description still mentions competing on leaderboard although Sidebar exposes it only for admin (`src/components/Sidebar.tsx:73`). Verify actual privacy intent against backend, without changing access rules from copy alone.

For **each** navigation item propose mouse, keyboard, active marker, AR/EN label, collapsed/mobile menu, direct load, allowed/denied role, and fallback on lazy-module/network failure. Sidebar link expressions retain references to legacy role flags, but those flags currently all resolve to the same super_admin boolean. No distinct legacy-role access has been assumed.

## Proposed cross-cutting evidence requirements (not execution)

1. Every writable row: isolated QA_TEST_ fixture ownership, before/after persistence after refresh, direct endpoint/RLS denial for member and visitor, cancellation/no write, network error and repeated click. No writes to production or real user rows.
2. Every row: desktop/tablet/mobile, keyboard/focus, Arabic/English and RTL, console/network errors, empty/loading/failed state, no random image/data fallback presented as real saved content. Shared profile and shell controls must be linked from the master matrix rather than counted as absent here.
3. Data boundaries requiring real integrations remain **Needs configuration / Not run**, not automatic pass: Google Drive OAuth/provider quotas, unpaid Gemini, Google Sheets service account, password-recovery delivery, camera hardware and 2 GiB transfer. Test fixtures/mock provider tests must be separately labeled from end-to-end provider success.
4. No pagination/sort/reorder/upload controls are assumed beyond those explicitly inventoried: Staff/Gallery/Projects/Blog/Events/Messages source currently has no general search/sort/pagination control; form builder uses up/down instead of drag reorder; CourseUnits has no delete control. Missing optional capabilities are a product decision, not an invented failing test.

Inventory created by admin source-inspection subtask only. The parent agent remains the only test executor and owner of the final consolidated matrix.

# Annex: Authentication, media and integrations detail

# Authentication, profile, media and integrations inventory

Date: 2026-10-15. STEP 1 read-only source inventory. **Every item below is Not executed in this audit.** A source contract or an existing test name is not a pass. No browser, provider, database, package install, test execution, secret file, `.env`, or `.runtime` content was accessed for this inventory.

Source references are repository-relative. The final matrix must expand each interaction into 1440/768/375 × Arabic/English × applicable role cases and record actual evidence independently. Existing reports concern previous runs and must not be promoted to current results.

## Decisions and isolation prerequisites

| ID | Needs decision / isolation prerequisite | Source / consequence |
|---|---|---|
| DEC-INT-01 | Requested administrative role cases conflict with a two-role application model. Confirm whether legacy `hr`, `event_manager`, `content_manager`, `editor` should intentionally be ordinary members or require a new permissions product design. Do not silently grant them permissions. | `src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx:69` maps `admin`/`super_admin` to UI `super_admin`, every other DB value to `member`; capability flags all alias `isSuperAdmin`; `src/lib/roleDefinitions.ts:54` exposes only two roles. Test denial separately from any requested positive role workflow. |
| DEC-INT-02 | Typed OTP is requested but profile implements email links. Confirm link-based flow is intended or whether OTP UI is a new feature. | `src/pages/Profile.tsx:74`, `:124`; `ResetPassword.tsx:20`; `VerifyEmail.tsx:33`. No profile `verifyOtp` input found. Wrong/expired OTP cannot be represented as a present UI flow. Wrong/expired emailed links still require testing. |
| DEC-INT-03 | Confirm independent-tab login expectation. Current sessions persist on refresh in one tab but are stored in `sessionStorage`, not shared localStorage. | `src/lib/supabase.ts:21`; `AuthContext.tsx:239`. New tab unauthenticated can be the intended policy rather than a bug. |
| DEC-INT-04 | Local OAuth/confirmation/QR canonical URLs need explicit isolated domain settings. Default canonical fallback goes to production even on localhost. | `src/lib/siteUrl.ts:9`; `server/courseMedia.ts:7`. Configure `VITE_SITE_URL` (and server `COURSE_SITE_URL` if needed) for approved local/staging redirect; allowlist this callback in the isolated Auth provider. Never follow default production URL during isolated tests. |
| DEC-INT-05 | Real provider tests require isolated Google OAuth client/account, Drive space and files, service-account Sheets access, Gemini key/free quota and a test inbox. No credentials were inspected or assumed present. | Default adapter tests cannot prove remote Google consent, delivery, quotas, refresh longevity, media transfer, Sheets semantics or runtime availability. User has authorized isolated QA accounts; missing provider credentials remain explicitly blocked. |
| DEC-INT-06 | Identify acceptable large-transfer and load-test budget/window on isolated services. Validate 2 GiB boundary without claiming 2 GiB transfer; real 1000 concurrent media users require a separate load environment and resource approval. | `server/courseMedia.ts:4`, `courseMediaService.ts:8`; free Drive links may throttle. Never stress production. |
| DEC-INT-07 | Do not use the ordinary dev command with inherited production environment. | `scripts/dev-server.ts:16` explicitly loads `.env`; source `vite.config.ts` loads env too. Build/start isolation must override every relevant public/server variable and prevent production egress. |

# Backend inventory — STEP 1

Date: 2026-10-15 (Africa/Cairo). **Every proposed case is Pending approval.** Static source inventory only: no tests, requests, database/schema changes, secret reads or deployments performed. Source references are repository-relative file:line coordinates. This inventory does not assert the deployed database's effective state.

## Counts

10 HTTP handlers, 2 shared API helpers, 2 server modules; 24 timestamped migrations; 33 unique public tables, 49 unique function names including legacy/internal helpers, 18 historically declared trigger names (one explicitly dropped later), 1 explicit public view, 4 storage buckets, 13 public tables explicitly added to realtime. Exact identifier/source index follows below. Alternate combined_migration.sql, full_schema.sql and seed.sql are not independent additional schemas.

## Authorization and shared case expansion

`api/_lib/authorize.ts:4` uses Auth getUser and persisted profiles role/status: confirmed active admin/super_admin only. Missing config503; missing/invalid bearer401; nonadmin/inactive/unconfirmed403; profile-read failure503. `supabase/migrations/20261013000001_real_operations.sql:4` is_admin matches this predicate. require_active_user at line11 checks active status but not confirmed email.

Profiles CHECK supports only user/admin (`20261010000004_phase2_schema_hardening.sql:52`, `20261013000000_missing_schema.sql:9`). `api/admin/users.ts:20,24` accepts member/user/admin/super_admin but stores user/admin. Frontend maps admin to super_admin and others member (`src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx:75,88`). **hr, event_manager, content_manager, editor and distinct super_admin permissions: Needs decision.** Test unsupported/forged roles cannot gain authority; do not invent their grants.

Apply to every action: guest, expired/malformed token, active member A/B, inactive/pending/unconfirmed member, admin A/B, inactive/unconfirmed admin, forged role metadata/claim, unsupported roles, missing/deleted profile; own/other/missing/malformed IDs; wrong methods, missing/null/array/string body, unknown action, DB/network/provider failure. Assert denied requests perform no writes/provider side effects; capture sanitized response plus DB before/after. Also test repeat, concurrent repeat, partial failure, rollback and retry where writes occur.
